- **HealthReviewer**: Mandatory quality gate. Runs health check + tests. Classifies critical vs warning. Blocks if critical found. Always executes. ([Typeir/Ikuisuus](https://github.com/Typeir/Ikuisuus/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/health-reviewer.agent.md))
- **healthtech-arch**: Especialista em regulamentação brasileira de saúde — ANS (RN 305 prontuário eletrônico), CFM (1.821/2007 prontuário, 2.314/2022 telemedicina, MP 2.200-2 prescrição ICP), TISS/TUSS (comunicação operadora), LGPD Art. 11 (dado sensível). Use ANTES de implementar feature que toca prontuário, telemedicina, prescrição digital, integração com operadora de plano de saúde, agenda médica ou aplicativo de paciente. NÃO substitui advogado/consultor médico — orienta na arquitetura. ([roldaobatista/roldao-method](https://github.com/roldaobatista/roldao-method/blob/HEAD/addons/healthtech-br/.claude/agents/healthtech-arch.md))
- **healthtech-fhir**: Healthtech BR/global com padrões FHIR R4/R5, HL7 v2, prontuário eletrônico (PEP/EHR), CFM 1821/2299 (telemedicina), LGPD art. 11 (dados sensíveis de saúde). SMART on FHIR + OAuth 2.0 com PKCE, scopes granulares (`patient/*.read`, `user/*.*`), audit via Provenance, consentimento via Consent resource. Falha aqui é responsabilidade civil + sanção CFM + multa LGPD (até 2% faturamento). ([pretinhuu1-boop/blindar](https://github.com/pretinhuu1-boop/blindar/blob/HEAD/agents/healthtech-fhir.md))
- **healthy-chef**: Primary recipe agent for healthy, batch-cook, make-ahead, and gut-friendly meals. Applies health constraint defaults (hard avoids, soft blockers, macro defaults, sodium targets, freezer-first design) and generates Nutrition Snapshots. Switch to this agent (or @-mention it) for health-oriented or batch-prep recipe requests. ([JustAnotherSoftwareDeveloper/home-cooking](https://github.com/JustAnotherSoftwareDeveloper/home-cooking/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/healthy-chef.md))
- **healthy-options-writer**: Options list writer subagent for healthy-chef. Given a research packet, active health constraints, and occasion directives, emits a correctly formatted options list with health-constraint-aware Why it fits lines. Invoked by healthy-chef via task delegation. ([JustAnotherSoftwareDeveloper/home-cooking](https://github.com/JustAnotherSoftwareDeveloper/home-cooking/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/healthy-options-writer.md))
- **healthy-recipe-writer**: Recipe writer subagent for healthy-chef. Given a selected option, research packet, and active health constraints, emits a correctly formatted full recipe with required Nutrition Snapshot and Reheat Plan. Invoked by healthy-chef via task delegation. ([JustAnotherSoftwareDeveloper/home-cooking](https://github.com/JustAnotherSoftwareDeveloper/home-cooking/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/healthy-recipe-writer.md))
- **heap-allocation-scanner**: .NET 10 서버 hot path의 불필요한 힙 할당(boxing, 루프 내 new, LINQ, 클로저 캡처, string 연산)을 탐지하는 에이전트. ([BerryBless/WebProject](https://github.com/BerryBless/WebProject/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/heap-allocation-scanner.md))
- **heap-analyst**: Works a heap dump end to end and returns only the findings — what is holding the memory, with class names, retained bytes and the GC-root paths that make each claim checkable. Delegate a whole heap question to it rather than reading histograms and dominator trees in the main conversation. It reports figures; it never maps them to source, edits anything, or decides what to change. ([petrbouda/jeffrey](https://github.com/petrbouda/jeffrey/blob/HEAD/build/plugin/agents/heap-analyst.md) · ★81 · 3 copies)
- **heap-analyst**: Specialist for heap dump analysis — retained sizes, dominator tree, GC root paths, known leak detectors, graph-based clusters, collection waste, duplicate subgraphs, heap-to-heap diffs, and correlating retained objects with JFR allocation sites. Dispatch for any .hprof file, OutOfMemoryError, or memory that never comes back after GC. ([btraceio/jafar-perf-box](https://github.com/btraceio/jafar-perf-box/blob/HEAD/plugins/jafar-perf/agents/heap-analyst.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **heapviz-and-agents**: `heapviz` is a small companion program that runs on your computer. It opens a trace there and serves it to this page, and to an AI assistant, over an authenticated connection to your own machine. Nothing is uploaded. ([bar4488/heap-visualizer](https://github.com/bar4488/heap-visualizer/blob/HEAD/src/web/guide/agents/heapviz-and-agents.md) · ★2)

- **hearing-prep**: ALWAYS invoke this agent to prepare a client-hearing question list from a rough project concept. Do not write the question list directly without invoking this agent. Trigger: "watch the listing", "any new filings", "court check", "what's due", or on schedule. ([uk-agents/uk-legal-plugins](https://github.com/uk-agents/uk-legal-plugins/blob/HEAD/litigation-legal-uk/agents/hearing-watcher.md) · ★19)

- **helper-agents-guide**: AutoBot includes various helper agents that provide specialized assistance for different tasks. These agents work behind the scenes to enhance user productivity and system functionality. ([mrveiss/AutoBot-AI](https://github.com/mrveiss/AutoBot-AI/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/helper-agents-guide.md) · ★11)
- **helper-author**: Owns the shared Cypress helper library for component automation. Annotates helpers with @tj tags, extracts/fixes shared helpers (e.g. waitForDropSettle, selector bugs), and regenerates the header blocks + type-helper-index. Use when a config type has no helper, a helper needs a DOM fix, or annotations/index drift. ([ToolJet/ToolJet](https://github.com/ToolJet/ToolJet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-author.md) · ★41k)
- **helper-census**: Research-team helper on the smallest model. Mechanical inventories for one job card - file lists and counts, trial-log totals, manifest listings, data spans and row counts. Read-only except its output file. Use it instead of spending a strong model on counting. ([kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena](https://github.com/kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena/blob/HEAD/skills/ai-research-team/assets/agents/helper-census.md) · ★1)
- **helper-curator**: Validate duplicate-helper candidates with Graphify and Serena and move proven shared behavior to the correct common module. ([CountJung/KISAutoTrade](https://github.com/CountJung/KISAutoTrade/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/helper-curator.agent.md))
- **helper-data**: Research-team helper on the smallest model. Public data jobs - runs the project's fetch tool for the exact instrument, window and output directory of one job card, then verifies sha256 manifests, row counts and gaps. Spawned only by the platform engineer, the coordinator or the judge, never by authors. ([kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena](https://github.com/kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena/blob/HEAD/skills/ai-research-team/assets/agents/helper-data.md) · ★1)
- **helper-draft**: Delegate drafting jobs to this agent in tests. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/scripts/validate/good/claude/agents/helper-draft.md) · ★3)
- **helper-draft**: Use to produce notes, summaries, explanations, research write-ups and edits that the caller specifies, writing only to the paths the caller names. Not for text in the user's own voice that leaves the computer (use ghostwriter) and not for critique (use helper-review). ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-draft.md) · ★3)
- **helper-fable-low**: Fable 5.2 at low effort: Cory's 11-12 trial on real work, to see if Fable's own intelligence needs less thinking (cheaper, faster). Judged on cause found, fix green first try, time and cost against Opus at high; a miss is rerun at medium. ([IssisX/ScraperX](https://github.com/IssisX/ScraperX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-fable-low.md) · ★1)
- **helper-high**: The integrator's Opus helper at high effort for an ordinary non-physics job (Cory 11-13: picked per job; plain `helper` runs at the session's effort): UI, tools, the switchboard, CI, sound, notes, long reads (Cory 11-06 11:30 AM). Physics, forces, her limits, world geometry and protected test changes go to helper-physics instead. A job it fails (a red proof it cannot fix, or a real review finding) goes again on helper-max. ([IssisX/ScraperX](https://github.com/IssisX/ScraperX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-high.md) · ★1)
- **helper-high**: ModelPilot helper mode at high effort. Use only when a ModelPilot helper-mode note names it. ([louiskoide/ModelPilot](https://github.com/louiskoide/ModelPilot/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/helper-high.md))
- **helper-judgement**: Use only for named judgement passes: thesis options, the devil's advocate lens, consolidating a critique round, voice-profile calibration, self-build safety review of a medium or high risk proposal. Writes at most the one file the caller names. If it cannot run (plan limits), the main session does the pass and says so. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-judgement.md) · ★3)
- **helper-judgement**: Delegate one judgement pass to this agent in tests. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/scripts/validate/good/claude/agents/helper-judgement.md) · ★3)
- **helper-lite**: Haiku, the lighter tier of `helper`: mechanical work with no judgment, fully spelled out by the brief — counts, fields and lines out of a transcript, log or json; 5+ screenshots to text; a rename, string swap or value table across named files. Returns the answer or the files touched, plus the command that proves it. Debugging, verifying, waits or anything the brief leaves open go to `helper` or the caller. ([dvakatsiienko/frame](https://github.com/dvakatsiienko/frame/blob/HEAD/home/.claude/agents/helper-lite.md) · 2 copies)
- **helper-low**: ModelPilot helper mode at low effort. Use only when a ModelPilot helper-mode note names it. ([louiskoide/ModelPilot](https://github.com/louiskoide/ModelPilot/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/helper-low.md))
- **helper-max**: The same Opus helper at max effort, for a job that failed on helper or helper-physics (a red proof it could not fix, or a real review finding). The second step of CLAUDE.md's settled ladder. ([IssisX/ScraperX](https://github.com/IssisX/ScraperX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-max.md) · ★1)
- **helper-max**: ModelPilot helper mode at max effort. Use only when a ModelPilot helper-mode note names it. ([louiskoide/ModelPilot](https://github.com/louiskoide/ModelPilot/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/helper-max.md))
- **helper-medium**: ModelPilot helper mode at medium effort. Use only when a ModelPilot helper-mode note names it. ([louiskoide/ModelPilot](https://github.com/louiskoide/ModelPilot/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/helper-medium.md))
- **helper-physics**: The integrator's Opus helper at xhigh effort for one physics job: physics, contacts, forces, her force/power/mass/leg-travel/launch limits, world geometry, or a protected test change (Cory 11-06 11:30 AM: these misses can pass tests, so they get more thought). A job it fails (a red proof it cannot fix, or a real review finding) goes again on helper-max. ([IssisX/ScraperX](https://github.com/IssisX/ScraperX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-physics.md) · ★1)
- **helper-preferences**: `HelperPreferencesService` is presentation policy, never an identity, persona, tool, or authorization authority. A host creates `HelperContext` from its authenticated request and supplies `authorize`, `resolvePolicy`, registered `styleIds`, and `validateOffering`. Browser payloads never supply those values. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/chat/agents/helper-preferences.md) · ★7)
- **helper-review**: Use for a blind, read-only review through one lens: critique, verification, QA, fact-check or rubric grading of a deliverable. Give it only file paths and the path of one lens brief; it returns one structured report. Run several in parallel for a critique round. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-review.md) · ★3)
- **helper-review**: Delegate read-only reviews to this agent in tests. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/scripts/validate/good/claude/agents/helper-review.md) · ★3)
- **helper-runner**: Research-team helper on the smallest model. Runs exactly the commands of one job card (unit tests, linters, validators, evaluator runs on development data) and returns the result lines. Use it for routine runs a team role has already specified; it never chooses what to run. ([kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena](https://github.com/kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena/blob/HEAD/skills/ai-research-team/assets/agents/helper-runner.md) · ★1)
- **helper-scout**: Read-only, advisory helper discovery. Lists existing project helpers a task could reuse, or a diff reinvented. ([qgadrian/aidrian](https://github.com/qgadrian/aidrian/blob/HEAD/plugins/aidrian/agents/helper-scout.md) · ★2)
- **helper-scribe**: Research-team helper on the smallest model. Formats already-verified rows given in the job card into Markdown tables, BOARD rows, channel-entry drafts or JSON; it adds no facts. Use it for formatting work after the numbers are checked. ([kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena](https://github.com/kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena/blob/HEAD/skills/ai-research-team/assets/agents/helper-scribe.md) · ★1)
- **helper-security-reviewer**: Adversarial security review of any change touching the privileged helper (Sources/XCodeVaultHelper, Sources/XCodeVaultHelperProtocol, XPC client code, launchd plists, signing scripts). Use before merging such changes. Must NOT be the agent that authored the change. ([pabloguia/XCodeVault](https://github.com/pabloguia/XCodeVault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-security-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **helper-surface**: The helper surface is the set of core files an adopter's checks and tests may import from outside the plugin, or run as commands: `scripts/lib/helper-surface.json` lists them, the names each exports, and each command's argv (#457, #655). An adopter holds one core file in its own tree, `scripts/lib/resolve-core.js`, which init and upgrade write, as its husky hooks run through it (#1839). Where it h ([lucmoseley/workflow-core](https://github.com/lucmoseley/workflow-core/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/helper-surface.md))
- **helper-triage**: Delegate sorting and scoring jobs to this agent in tests. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/scripts/validate/good/claude/agents/helper-triage.md) · ★3)
- **helper-triage**: Use for small, mechanical judgement on one item or a short batch of files that the caller names by path (classify, tag, score, extract fields, summarise one email or advert). Returns JSON or a short list only. Read-only and cheap; not for drafting or critique. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-triage.md) · ★3)
- **helper-verify**: Research-team helper on the smallest model. Adversarial claim checker - takes a batch of claims with cited evidence (path:line, numbers in a report against result files) and returns CONFIRMED, REFUTED or UNVERIFIABLE per claim after opening each cited location. Read-only. Use it before a report, a decision or a merge relies on numbers. ([kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena](https://github.com/kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena/blob/HEAD/skills/ai-research-team/assets/agents/helper-verify.md) · ★1)
- **helper-worker**: Use this agent for quick lookups, repo reconnaissance, and evidence gathering before editing: finding where something is defined, checking how a dependency is used, confirming a config value, or summarizing a subsystem's structure. It is read-only and fast — route here when you need facts before deciding what to change, not when you need deep root-cause analysis (use forensic-analyst) or a judgment-heavy review (use reviewer). ([fuentesjr/autobots](https://github.com/fuentesjr/autobots/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-worker.md))
- **helper-xhigh**: ModelPilot helper mode at xhigh effort. Use only when a ModelPilot helper-mode note names it. ([louiskoide/ModelPilot](https://github.com/louiskoide/ModelPilot/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/helper-xhigh.md))
- **helper.agent**: Helper agent for plugin functionality ([microsoft/apm](https://github.com/microsoft/apm/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/mock-plugin/.apm/agents/helper.agent.md) · ★4.4k · 4 copies)
- **helper.agent**: General helper ([redhat-community-ai-tools/harness-eval](https://github.com/redhat-community-ai-tools/harness-eval/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/sample-copilot-setup/.github/agents/helper.agent.md) · ★35)
- **helper.agent**: Helper agent with a mostly explicit tool list ([toonight/Airworthy](https://github.com/toonight/Airworthy/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/mixed/.github/agents/helper.agent.md))
- **helpers**: `client.beta.agents.sessions.stream()` submits input to an idle session and returns a single-use async iterable. Iteration opens the event subscription before submitting input. The session must have a single input writer while the helper runs because the input endpoint does not return a turn ID. ([NickCollect/ai-coding-runbook](https://github.com/NickCollect/ai-coding-runbook/blob/HEAD/01_Raw/github/openai/openai-node/docs/agents/helpers.md) · ★8)
- **Helpers**: &gt; 질문: 헬퍼 함수와 번역 단위 전용 타입은 어디에 두고 어떻게 이름 붙이는가? &gt; 독자: 헬퍼를 더하거나 `.cpp` 안의 타입을 정리하는 사람. &gt; 결론: 먼저 주인 타입을 찾는다(그 타입의 멤버). 주인이 없을 때만 여러 번역 단위가 쓰면 헤더의 `XxxUtil`, 한 `.cpp` 전용이면 그 파일의 익명 네임스페이스(`XxxInternal`)다. ([sswgame/LearningTemplate](https://github.com/sswgame/LearningTemplate/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/Helpers.md) · ★2)

- **herdr-control**: For coordinator or worker startup, resume, or model changes in Herdr or Herdr Projects, read [model selection](model-selection.md) before choosing the route. ([nathanvale/dotfiles-private](https://github.com/nathanvale/dotfiles-private/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-control.md))
- **herdr-core**: herdr-core (`hc`) is the background program that owns every terminal and agent session; Herdr.app is the window that shows them. They find each other through one Unix socket. This page is for an agent that has to check or talk to that core. The design is [docs/designs/herdr-core-socket-cutover.md](../designs/herdr-core-socket-cutover.md). ([ray-manaloto/dotfiles](https://github.com/ray-manaloto/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-core.md))
- **herdr-cto**: Herdr team CTO responsible for architecture, interfaces, tradeoffs, and technical risk. ([DiQiTech-AI/teamcraft](https://github.com/DiQiTech-AI/teamcraft/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/herdr-cto.md) · ★2)
- **herdr-custom**: Herdr team custom specialist whose concrete responsibility comes from the task prompt. ([DiQiTech-AI/teamcraft](https://github.com/DiQiTech-AI/teamcraft/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/herdr-custom.md) · ★2)
- **herdr-designer**: Herdr team designer responsible for product flows, interaction, and visual specifications. ([DiQiTech-AI/teamcraft](https://github.com/DiQiTech-AI/teamcraft/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/herdr-designer.md) · ★2)
- **herdr-developer**: Herdr team developer responsible for bounded implementation and verification. ([DiQiTech-AI/teamcraft](https://github.com/DiQiTech-AI/teamcraft/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/herdr-developer.md) · ★2)
- **herdr-fleet-monitor**: Read-only triage of a herdr agent fleet. Use to find which agents are blocked, done-but-unreviewed, or stuck, and to explain why a herdr agent's detected state looks wrong. Inspects and reports only — never sends input, spawns, or restarts agents. ([jbaham2/herdr-plugin](https://github.com/jbaham2/herdr-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/herdr-fleet-monitor.md) · ★1)
- **herdr-orchestrator**: Sets up a herdr workspace and dispatches a fleet of coding agents to work a multi-part task in parallel, then coordinates their handoffs. Use when a task should be split across several agents in herdr. Spawns and assigns agents (without stealing focus) and wires up wait-coordination between them. ([jbaham2/herdr-plugin](https://github.com/jbaham2/herdr-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/herdr-orchestrator.md) · ★1)
- **herdr-pane-message**: Use when Codex needs to send a message, instruction, question, or finding to another agent session in the same Herdr workspace. ([motioneso/moss](https://github.com/motioneso/moss/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-pane-message.md) · ★3)
- **herdr-projects**: Use the installed `herdr-projects` skill and its full CLI path. Current CLI help owns syntax; `details` and `show` own task state and effective settings. Read app-generated coordinator or task rules supplied to this session. ([nathanvale/dotfiles-private](https://github.com/nathanvale/dotfiles-private/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-projects.md))
- **herdr-sidebar-metadata**: 複数の Claude Code エージェントを Herdr で並走させると、「どのペインが plan モードで 止まっているのか」「どれが bypass で走っているのか」がサイドバーから読めない。 Herdr が自前で検出するのは agent_status(working/blocked/…)とターミナルタイトル だけで、Claude Code 側の permission mode・モデル・context 使用率は Herdr のデータ モデルに存在しないからだ。 ([tarotene/dotfiles](https://github.com/tarotene/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-sidebar-metadata.md) · ★1)

- **herdr-tab-bar-local**: Herdr のタブバー右側(`ui.tab_bar_right`)には、claude-usage(`docs/claude/claude-usage.md`)の ほかに、ホストごとに中身が違う 1 行を出したい場面がある。その中身は、このリポジトリ(public)に 名前を書けない道具が作ることがある(ADR-0034: 私的な実値は書かない、ADR-0025: 自作の pre-release CLI の存在を書かない)。 ([tarotene/dotfiles](https://github.com/tarotene/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-tab-bar-local.md) · ★1)

For Desktop portal/coordinator routes, invoke that skill even without an explicit Herdr mention and permit control outside `HERDR_ENV=1`. This overrides only its mention trigger and environment refusal; keep its reviewed payload intact. Board operations use [their own route](herdr-projects.md). ([nathanvale/dotfiles-private](https://github.com/nathanvale/dotfiles-private/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-terminal.md))

- **herdr-ui-overhaul-execution**: This document is retained as execution history for UI-overhaul work that used Herdr during Plans 052–054. It no longer governs future UI-overhaul plans. Plans 055–059 intentionally specify product scope, atomic commits, dependencies, rollback, proof, and owner gates without prescribing execution mechanics. The [evidence protocol](implementation-evidence.md) remains the active acceptance and review ([pedrochagasmaster/repforge](https://github.com/pedrochagasmaster/repforge/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-ui-overhaul-execution.md))
- **herdr-version-upgrade**: Audit every stable Herdr release (`v*` tags in [`herdrdev/herdr`](https://github.com/herdrdev/herdr/releases)), including patches. Skip preview builds. Herdr is not an npm dependency: our packages call the `herdr` CLI found on `PATH`, so a Herdr release breaks them at runtime, not at install time or during a typecheck. An audit causes a release only when a decision changes a package. ([HenryQW/pi-harness](https://github.com/HenryQW/pi-harness/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/herdr-version-upgrade.md) · ★21 · 2 copies)
- **herdr-worker**: Worker agent for herdr orchestrated tasks. Executes a specific subtask within strict file boundaries, reports results in standardized format. ([cheolmin/herdr-orchestrator](https://github.com/cheolmin/herdr-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/herdr-worker.md))
- **herdr-worker**: Use this agent to run one bounded task through one coding agent in herdr (claude, codex, agy or another harness) and get back a fixed report with receipts, without filling the main session's context with screens. Typical triggers: a task-scenario step ("have a codex in herdr fix the failing test in &lt;repo&gt; and report"), a one-off check by another harness in herdr ("ask a claude in herdr whether this config parses"), or a delegated read-only review of one diff by a herdr agent. Not for multi-agent work or anything that needs the user before it starts. It never asks the user; a decision it canno… ([yigitkonur/herdr-conductor](https://github.com/yigitkonur/herdr-conductor/blob/HEAD/plugins/herdr-conductor/agents/herdr-worker.md))
- **HERDR_USAGE**: **Layout.** One workspace per orchestrated run (unless the human explicitly requests another topology). **One tab per task**, named `&lt;task&gt; - &lt;description&gt;` (e.g. `t101 - routing refinement`). All sessions pertaining to that task open inside its tab and are named `&lt;task&gt;_&lt;role&gt;` (e.g. `t107_executor`, `t107_reviewer`, `t111_researcher`, `t105_interlocutor`).
